           Lease modifications                                         租賃修改

           The Group accounts for a lease modification                 在下列情況下，本集團將租賃
           as a separate lease if:                                     修改作為單獨租賃入賬：

           –      the modification increases the scope of              －    該修改通過增加使用一項
                  the lease by adding the right to use one                  或多項相關資產的權利擴
                  or more underlying assets; and                            大租賃範圍；及

           –      the consideration for the leases                     －    租賃代價增加的金額相當
                  increases by an amount commensurate                       於範圍擴大對應的獨立價
                  with the stand-alone price for the                        格，及按照特定合約的情
                  increase in scope and any appropriate                     況對獨立價格進行的任何
                  adjustments to that stand-alone price                     適當調整。
                  to reflect the circumstances of the
                  particular contract.

           For a lease modification that is not accounted              就未作為單獨租賃入賬的租賃
           for as a separate lease, the Group remeasures               修改而言，本集團根據經修改
           the lease liability based on the lease term of              租賃的租期，通過於該修改生
           the modified lease by discounting the revised               效日期使用經修訂貼現率貼現
           lease payments using a revised discount rate                經修訂租賃付款重新計量租賃
           at the effective date of the modification.                  負債。

                     For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for              就計量使用公允價值模式計量
                     investment properties that are measured using               的投資物業的遞延稅項而言，
                     the fair value model, the carrying amounts of               該等物業的賬面值被假設將透
                     such properties are presumed to be recovered                過出售全部收回，惟該假設遭
                     entirely through sale, unless the presumption               駁回則除外。倘投資物業可進
                     is rebutted. The presumption is rebutted when               行折舊並目標為將投資物業所
                     the investment property is depreciable and is               包含的絕大部分經濟利益隨時
                     held within a business model whose objective                間逐步消耗而非透過出售消耗
                     is to consume substantially all of the economic             的商業模式持有，則該假設將
                     benefits embodied in the investment property                遭駁回。
                     over time, rather than through sale.

                     For the purposes of measuring deferred                      就計量集團確認使用權資產和
                     tax for leasing transactions in which the                   相關租賃負債的租賃交易的遞
                     Group recognises the right-of-use assets and                延稅，本集團首先確定減稅是
                     the related lease liabilities, the Group first              歸於使用權資產還是租賃負債。
                     determines whether the tax deductions are
                     attributable to the right-of-use assets or the
                     lease liabilities.

                     For leasing transactions in which the tax                   就稅項扣減歸屬於租賃負債的
                     deductions are attributable to the lease                    租賃交易而言，本集團就整體
                     liabilities, the Group applies HKAS 12 Income               租賃交易應用香港會計準則第
                     Taxes requirements to the leasing transaction               12號所得稅的規定。使用權資
                     as a whole. Temporary differences relating                  產及租賃負債的暫時性差異按

38 CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                 38 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities       本集團管理其資本以確保本集團旗下
   in the Group will be able to continue as a going            實體在保持持續經營之同時，通過優
   concern while maximising the return to shareholders         化債務與股本之平衡為股東帶來最大
   through the optimisation of the debt and equity             回報。本集團之整體策略相比前一年
   balance. The Group’s overall strategy remains               未有變化。
   unchanged from prior year.

   The capital structure of the Group consists of lease       本集團之資本結構包括租賃負債、銀
   liabilities, bank and other borrowings and equity          行及其他借款及本公司股東應佔權益
   attributable to owners of the Company, comprising          （包括已發行股本、累計溢利及其他儲
   issued share capital, accumulated profits and other        備）。
   reserves.

   The directors of the Company review the capital             本公司董事每半年審核資本結構，作
   structure on a semi-annual basis. As part of this           為審核一部分，本公司董事會考慮資
   review, the directors of the Company consider the           本成本與各類資本的風險，基於本公
   cost of capital and the risks associates with each          司董事建議基礎上，本集團將通過支
   class of capital. Based on recommendations of the           付股息、發行新股及發行新債或贖回
   directors of the Company, the Group will balance            現有債務以平衡整體資本結構。
   its overall capital structure through the payment of
   dividends, new share issues as well as the issue of
   new debt or the redemption of existing debt.

                     ventures and other receivables. The Group                  持有任何抵押品或其他信用增
                     does not hold any collateral or other credit               級措施以保障其金融資產有關
